| roar | *vi. & n.* 吼叫；咆哮 |
| individual | *adj.* 单独的；个别的<br>*n.* 个人<br>✓【派】individually *adv.* 分别地；单独地 |
| chief | *adj.* 最重要的；最高级别的<br>*n.* （公司或机构的）首领；酋长<br>✓ chief editor 总编辑<br>　chief engineer 总工程师；总技师 |
| nearby | *adj.* 附近的；邻近的<br>*adv.* 在附近 |
| military | *adj.* 军事的；军用的 |
| legal | *adj.* 法律的；合法的<br>✓【反】illegal *adj.* 非法的；违法的；不合法的<br>　【派】legally *adv.* 合法地；法律上 |
| fascinating | *adj.* 极有吸引力的；迷人的<br>✓ fascinate *v.* 深深吸引；迷住<br>　fascination *n.* 魅力；入迷 |
| generous | *adj.* 慷慨的；大方的；丰富的<br>✓ be generous with… 不吝惜……<br>　【派】generously *adv.* 慷慨地；大方地<br>　generosity *n.* 慷慨；大方 |
| sensory | *adj.* 感觉的；感官的<br>✓ sense *vt.* 感觉到；意识到<br>　sensor *n.* 传感器 |
| striking | *adj.* 引人注目的；显著的<br>✓ strike *vt.* 撞；击；打；突然想到 |
| join… to… | 把……和……连接或联结起来 |
| keep your eyes open（for） | 留心；留意<br>✓ keep an eye on 照看；留意；留神 |

## 探究词汇 提高

## break away（from sb/sth）脱离；背叛；逃脱；突然改变

**教材原句 P40** Finally, in the 20th century, the southern part of Ireland **broke away from** the UK, which resulted in the full name we have today: the United Kingdom of Great Britain and Northern Ireland. 最后，在 20 世纪，爱尔兰南部脱离了联合王国，形成了我们现在的全称：大不列颠及北爱尔兰联合王国。

- In order to improve your study, you should first

break away from your bad study habits. 为了提高你的学习成绩，你首先应该改掉不良的学习习惯。

**联想拓展** 含有 break 的其他常用短语

break down（机器或车辆）出故障；垮掉；（谈判等）失败；情不自禁地哭起来；使分解（为）

break in 打断（说话）；闯入　break into 强行闯入

break off 中断；断开

break out（战争、火灾等）突然发生

break up（关系等）破裂；解散；（学校）期终放假

- He **broke down** as he thought of his failure to sell

all his papers. 当他想到他没有卖掉所有的报纸时，他崩溃了。

- We were discussing our plans, but had to **break off** when the telephone rang. 我们在讨论计划时电话突然响了，我们只好中断讨论。
- Dark clouds were gathering in the sky. War was soon to **break out**. 空中乌云密布，战争即将爆发。

## belong to 属于；是……的成员/一部分

**教材原句 P40** The four countries that **belong to** the United Kingdom work together in some areas. 同属于联合王国的四个国家在某些领域共同合作。

- He **belongs to** the golf club. 他是高尔夫球俱乐部的一员。

**特别注意** ①belong to 后面接名词或代词宾格。

②belong to 不用于被动语态和进行时态。

③belong to 作后置定语时，用 belonging to 或含有 belong to 的定语从句。

- Remember to take everything **belonging to you/that belongs to you** before leaving the restaurant. 离开餐馆之前记得带走所有属于你的东西。

**联想拓展** ①belong *vi.* 应在（某处）；适应

- At first, we didn't feel we **belong** here, but now we are attached to the land we live on. 起初，我们在这里感觉格格不入，但是现在我们依恋着我们所居住的这片土地。
- Put the books back where they **belong** after you have finished them. 你在看完之后把书放回原处。

②belongings *n.*［*pl.*］所有物；财物

## as well as 同（一样也）；和；还

**教材原句 P40** They use the same flag, known as the Union Jack, **as well as** share the same currency and military defence. 它们使用相同的旗帜——英国国旗，流通同一种货币并共同参与军事防御。

- She is a talented musician **as well as** a painter. 她不但是个画家，而且是个天才音乐家。

**特别注意** ①as well as 短语修饰主语时，谓语动词必须与前面的主语在人称和数上保持一致，不受 as well as 后面名词（词组）的影响。有相同用法的还有：with、along with、together with、besides、like、rather than、without、except、including、but、in addition to 等。

- Mr Chen, **as well as** his kids, **has** a good command of the English language. 陈先生和他的孩子都精通英语。

②as well as 置于句首相当于 in addition to，意为"除……之外还"。

- **As well as** going to the movies and playing football with my friends, I spend a lot of time with my pets. 除了和朋友一起看电影和踢足球，我还花很多时间和我的宠物在一起。

**联想拓展** as well 也；又；还（相当于 too，用于肯定句中，通常位于句尾）

- He is a teacher and a poet **as well**. 他是老师，也是诗人。

## defence *n.* 防御；保卫；防卫

**教材原句 P40** They use the same flag, known as the Union Jack, as well as share the same currency and military **defence**. 它们使用相同的旗帜——英国国旗，流通同一种货币并共同参与军事防御。

**常用搭配** in defence of… 为保卫……

in defence 防守

- He gave his life **in defence of** his country. 他为保卫祖国而献出了生命。
- She stepped back appearing surprised and put up her hands, as if **in defence**. 她向后退了几步，显得很惊讶，并举起双手，好像在自卫。

**联想词** defend *v.* 保护；保卫；防御

defend… against/from… 保卫……免受……

- White blood cells help **defend** the body **against** infection. 白细胞有助于保护身体免受感染。

---

---

<!-- page-090 -->


## surround *vt.* 围绕；包围

**教材原句 P40** Almost everywhere you go in the UK, you will be **surrounded** by evidence of four different groups of people who took over at different times throughout history. 不论你去到英国哪个地方，你几乎都能够在周围找到四个不同群体的痕迹，这四个群体曾在不同的历史时期占领过英国。

**常用搭配** sb/sth be surrounded by/with sth 某人/某物被另一物包围

surround oneself with sb/sth 喜欢结交（某类人）；喜欢身边总有（某类东西）

- The whole city **is surrounded by** water, making it very beautiful. 整座城市被水环绕，使得它非常美丽。
- People say it is Canada's most beautiful city, **surrounded by** mountains and the Pacific Ocean. 人们说它是加拿大最美丽的城市，被大山和太平洋环抱。
- I like to **surround myself with** beautiful things. 我喜欢身边老有漂亮的东西。[《牛津高阶》]

**联想词** ①surrounding *adj.* 周围的；附近的

- The **surrounding** villages have been absorbed by the growing city. 周围的村庄已经并入不断扩张的城市。

②surroundings *n.*［*pl.*］环境

- Mr White says he is heavily influenced by his **surroundings** and the people in them. 怀特先生说他深受周围环境及人的影响。

## evidence *n.*［U］证据；证明

**教材原句 P40** Almost everywhere you go in the UK, you will be surrounded by **evidence** of four different groups of people who took over at different times throughout history. 不论你去到英国哪个地方，你几乎都能够在周围找到四个不同群体的痕迹，这四个群体曾在不同的历史时期占领过英国。

**常见用法** in evidence 显眼；显而易见

there is evidence that… 有证据表明……

- So far, **there is no convincing evidence that** there exists life in outer space. 到目前为止，还没有令人信服的证据表明外太空存在生命。

**联想词** ①evident *adj.* 明显的；清楚的；明白的

②evidently *adv.* 明显地；据说

## charge

### ❶ *vt. & vi.* 充电；收费 *vt.* 控告；指责；使承担（任务、责任）

**释义**

①to pass electricity through sth so that it is stored there

②to ask an amount of money for goods or a service

③to accuse sb formally of a crime

④to give sb a responsibility or task

**教材原句 P42** Judy and I had our car parked in an underground car park near Trafalgar Square, where we could get our car battery **charged**. 我和朱迪把车停在了特拉法尔加广场附近的一个地下停车场，在那里我们可以给汽车电池充电。

**常用搭配** charge（sb）for sth 因某物/某事（向某人）收费

charge sb… for sth 因某事/某物向某人要价……

be charged with 被指控

charge sb with（doing）sth 指责某人（做）某事

- How much do you **charge for** your services? 你这些服务收费多少？
- He **was charged with** driving under the influence and failure to maintain the lane. 他被指控酒后驾驶和未按规定车道行驶。

### ❷ *n.* 收费；费用；指控；主管；负责；充电量

**常用搭配** in charge（of）主管；负责

in the charge of 由……负责

in one's charge 由……负责/主管

take charge（of）负责；掌管

free of charge 免费

- He asked to speak to the person **in charge**. 他要求和负责人说话。
- Could I speak to whoever is **in charge of** the international sales, please? 请问我能和负责国际销售的人说话吗？

## amount *n.* 数量；金额

**教材原句 P42** We found ourselves very surprised by the large number of visitors and the **amount** of noise at the entrance of the National Gallery. 国家美术馆入口处聚集了大批游客，人声鼎沸，这让我们感到非常惊讶。

**常见用法** "a large/an enormous/a huge amount of+不可数名词"，表示"大量的……"，作主语时，谓语动词用单数；"large/enormous/huge amounts of+不可数名词"，表示"大量的……"，作主语时，谓语动词用复数。

- **A large amount of damage has** been done by the earthquake. 地震造成了巨大的破坏。
- **Large amounts of money were** spent on the bridge. 大量的钱被花在这座桥上。

**联想拓展** 表示"许多"的短语归纳

- "许多"：
  - 常接可数名词复数：a large/great/good number of、a great/good many、a good few/quite a few
  - 作主语时，谓语动词用单数形式：many a+可数名词单数、more than one+可数名词单数
  - 常接不可数名词：a great/good deal of、a great/large amount of、quite a little
  - 后接可数名词和不可数名词均可：a lot of/lots of、a large/great quantity of、large quantities of、plenty of

## approach

### ❶ *vt. & vi.*（时间、距离上）靠近；接近

**释义**

to come near to sb/sth in distance or time

**教材原句 P42** It was hard to **approach** the painting as there were so many people around. 由于有许多人围着那幅画，我们很难靠近。

- The Spring Festival is **approaching**, and it's time to consider where we are going for a family vacation. 春节快到了，是时候考虑全家去哪里度假了。

**熟词生义** *vt.*（在数额、水平或质量上）接近；接洽；着手处理

**释义**

①to come close to sth in amount, level or quality

②to speak to sb about sth

③to start dealing with a problem, task, etc in a particular way

- Few writers **approach** his richness of language. 他语言之丰富很少有作家能望其项背。

### ❷ *n.* 方法；途径；（在距离或时间上的）接近

**释义**

①a way of doing or thinking about sth

②movement nearer to sb/sth in distance or time

**常用搭配** the approach of sth 某事的来临

an approach to（doing）sth（做）某事的方法/途径

- He took a very scientific **approach to** management. 他采取了一种非常科学的管理方法。

## eager *adj.* 热切的；渴望的

**教材原句 P43** I was **eager**/surprised to see/learn/hear that… 我想看/了解/听……；看到/了解到/听到……我感到惊讶。

**常用搭配** be eager to do sth 渴望做某事

be eager for sth 渴望得到某物

---

---

<!-- page-092 -->


- He **was so eager to expand** his farm that he did not notice the river flowing slowly towards his door. 他非常渴望扩大农场以至于他没有注意到河水慢慢地流向了他的家门。
- Kate was in a hurry to grow up, **eager for** knowledge and experience. 凯特急于长大，渴望获取知识和经验。

**联想词** ①eagerness *n.* 热切；渴望

②eagerly *adv.* 热切地；渴望地

- We are **eagerly** looking forward to your reply. 我们热切期待着你的回复。

## greet *vt.* 迎接；问候；对……作出反应

**教材原句 P44** On a quiet morning in the mountains, feel the sun on your skin, and

breathe in the sweet scent of fresh flowers while birds **greet** the new day with their morning song. 在山区宁静的清晨中，你可以感受阳光洒在肌肤上，呼吸鲜花的芳香，同时鸟儿用它们清晨的歌声迎接新一天的来临。

**常用搭配** greet sb with… 以……向某人问候；以……欢迎某人

be greeted with/by… 受到（某种反应）

greet the New Year 迎新年

- When it was first staged in 1949, the play **was greeted with** enthusiastic reviews. 该剧首次在 1949 年上演的时候，受到了热烈的评价。

**联想词** greeting *n.* 问候；致意；［*pl.*］祝词

New Year greetings 新年祝词

**差距词汇 拔尖**

**multicultural** *adj.* 多元文化的；多种文化融合的

Edinburgh, the capital of Scotland, is a multicultural mix of ancient and modern times, and is popular with tourists. 苏格兰的首府爱丁堡融合了古代和现代的多种文化，很受游客欢迎。

**reform** *n.* 改革；变革

Considering our country is a nation with more than two thousand years' education tradition, how not to lose tradition in meeting with the reform in the future is the most vital subject today. 鉴于我国是一个有两千多年教育传统的国家，如何在面向未来的变革中不丢失传统，是今天最重大的课题。

**be composed of** 由……组成

The settlers are composed of native English speakers together with recent immigrants. 定居者由以英语为母语的人和新移民组成。

**keen** *adj.* 热衷的

Despite various requests and proposals, the government is keen to retain the current system. 尽管提出了各种要求和建议，但政府仍热衷于保留当前的系统。

**settler** *n.*（到人烟稀少的新地区定居的）移民；殖民者

Sediment cores from the Bering Sea prove that the first human settlers of the New World spent thousands of years inhabiting Beringia. 白令海的沉积物岩芯证明了新大陆的第一批人类定居者在白令陆桥居住了数千年。

**representative** *n.* 代表；典型人物 *adj.* 典型的，有代表性的

Chinese Kung Fu is regarded as a traditional sport gaining more and more popularity and even stands as a representative of Chinese culture. 中国功夫被认为是一项越来越受欢迎的传统运动，甚至是中国文化的代表。

---

---

<!-- page-093 -->


**export** *n.* 输出，出口；出口商品 *v.* 出口

The famous Silk Road that came into existence around 200 BCE opened the gates for Chinese export of silk and clothing. 著名的"丝绸之路"在公元前 200 年左右出现，打开了中国丝绸和服装出口的大门。

**be bound to do** 一定会/很可能会做……

While tracing your family tree you are bound to discover ancestors who came to America from other countries. 在追溯你的家谱时，你一定会发现从其他国家来到美国的祖先。

# Part Ⅱ　句子分类探究

## 长难句分析

> **Almost everywhere you go in the UK, you will be surrounded by evidence of four different groups of people who took over at different times throughout history.**

（everywhere 引导地点状语从句：everywhere you go in the UK；主句：you will be surrounded by evidence of four different groups of people；who 引导定语从句，修饰 four different groups of people：who took over at different times throughout history）【P40】

**句意** 不论你去到英国哪个地方，你几乎都能够在周围找到四个不同群体的痕迹，这四个群体曾在不同的历史时期占领过英国。

> **The peaceful landscape of the "Emerald Isle" and its many green counties is a true feast for the eyes, with its rolling green hills dotted with sheep and cattle.**

（主语：The peaceful landscape of the "Emerald Isle" and its many green counties；系表结构：is a true feast for the eyes；"with+宾语+宾补"复合结构作状语，过去分词短语作宾补：with its rolling green hills dotted with sheep and cattle）【P44】

**句意** "绿宝石岛"风光宁静秀美，郡县草木葱茏，青山连绵起伏，牛羊点缀其中，着实让人大饱眼福。

> **With all this beauty, it is not surprising that Ireland has developed strong traditions that include music, dancing, and dining.**

（原因状语：With all this beauty；含有主语从句的复合句，it 为形式主语，that 从句为真正的主语；that 引导定语从句，修饰 traditions）【P44】

**句意** 爱尔兰是如此的美丽，在这样的环境中孕育出的音乐、舞蹈和餐饮等辉煌传统也就不足为奇了。

**知识拓展** **it+be+*adj.*+that 从句**（it 为形式主语，that 从句为真正的主语），常用于此句式的形容词有 clear、obvious、true、possible、certain、important、necessary、strange、essential 等。形容词为 important、necessary、essential 等时，that 从句通常用虚拟语气，即谓语为"should+动词原形"，should 可以省略。

- **It is obvious that** agriculture is fundamental to the development of economy. 很明显，农业是经济发展所必需的。

## 高频句式

**句式 1　if 省略结构**

if possible 如果可能的话　if so 如果是这样的话

if necessary 如果有必要的话

if not 如果不这样的话　if any 如果有的话

if ever 如果曾经发生过的话

**特别注意** 该结构中通常省略了"主语+系动词"。例如：if possible = if it is possible；if necessary = if it is necessary；if so = if it is so 等。

**链接课文** So what is the difference between them, **if any**? 如果它们之间存在差异的话，那究竟是哪些地方不一样呢？【P40】

**经典例句**

- The garbage is then taken away and, **if possible**, recycled. 然后垃圾被运走，如果可能的话，会被加以回收利用。
- We will have the old house pulled down **if necessary**. 如果有必要的话，我们将把旧房子拆掉。

---

---

<!-- page-094 -->


**句式 2　主语+be+*adj.*+to do sth**

此结构中，形容词多为表示心理活动的词，常见的有：surprised、moved、disappointed、pleased、happy、sad、delighted、sorry、interested、glad、worried 等。

**链接课文** If you keep your eyes open, you will be **surprised to find** that you can see both its past and its present. 如果留意，你会惊奇地发现你既可以看到它的过去也可以看到它的现在。【P41】

**经典例句**

- I was **delighted to see** my old friend Bessie again. 我很高兴又看到老朋友贝茜。
- Fascinated by the structural beauty of the Forbidden City as a painter, she is equally **interested to learn** more about Chinese philosophy. 作为一名画家，她对故宫的建筑之美着迷，同样，她也有兴趣更多了解中国哲学。

# Part Ⅲ　单元语法归纳

## 重点1　过去分词作定语

**教材原句**

①Most people just use the **shortened** name: "the United Kingdom" or "the UK". 大多数人只使用简称："the United Kingdom"或"the UK"。（过去分词 shortened 作前置定语，修饰 name，表示被动）【P40】

②They use the same flag, **known as the Union Jack**, as well as share the same currency and military defence. 它们使用相同的旗帜——英国国旗，流通同一种货币并共同参与军事防御。（过去分词短语 known as the Union Jack 作后置定语，修饰 flag，表示被动）【P40】

### ❶ 过去分词作定语时的位置

（1）单个的过去分词作定语时，位于被修饰词的前面。但修饰 something、everything、anything、nothing、somebody、nobody、those 等时，要放在这些词的后面。

- The **broken** glass has been thrown outside. 碎玻璃片已经被扔到外面去了。
- There's almost **nothing changed** since I left the town two years ago. 自从我两年前离开以来，这个城镇几乎没有什么变化。

（2）过去分词短语作定语时，位于被修饰词的后面。

- To return to the problem of water pollution, I'd like you to look at a study **conducted in Australia**

in 2012. 回到水污染的问题，我想要你们看一项 2012 年在澳大利亚进行的研究。

### ❷ 过去分词与现在分词作定语时的区别

（1）及物动词的过去分词作定语，在语态上表示被动，在时间上表示动作已经发生或完成，有时也不表示时间性。不及物动词的过去分词作定语，不表示被动意义，只强调动作已完成。

- The Student Union will contact the **chosen** candidates for an interview in a few days. 几天后，学生会将联系被选中的候选人进行面试。（chosen 作定语修饰 candidates，表被动和完成）
- With **fallen** leaves buried under the earth every year, the soil becomes richer and richer. 每年都有落叶埋在地里，因此土壤变得越来越肥沃。（fallen 作定语修饰 leaves，表完成）

（2）现在分词作定语表示主动或正在进行。

- I was the first Western TV reporter **permitted** to film a special unit **caring** for pandas **rescued** from starvation in the wild. 我是第一个被允许拍摄一个特别小组的西方电视台记者，这个小组照顾从野外营救回来的快饿死的熊猫。（caring… 作定语修饰 unit，表主动；permitted… 和 rescued… 作定语，分别修饰 reporter 和 pandas，表被动和完成）

- **developed** regions 发达地区（表完成）
- **developing** regions 发展中地区（表主动和正在进行）

- the English **spoken** today 如今所说的英语（与所修饰词 English 之间为逻辑上的被动关系）
- the students **speaking** English 说英语的学生（与所修饰词 students 之间为逻辑上的主动关系）

**特别注意** 有些过去分词或现在分词已形容词化，这类形容词中，过去分词转化的形容词作定语时，表示被修饰词所处的状态，意为"（感到）……的"；现在分词转化的形容词作定语时，表示被修饰词所具有的特征，意为"令人……的"。

- a **frightening** animal 令人害怕的动物
- a **frightened** animal 受到惊吓的动物

## 重点2　过去分词作宾语补足语

**教材原句**

①They had castles **built** all around England, and made changes to the legal system. 他们在英格兰各地建造城堡，并对法律体系进行了改革。（过去分词 built 作宾补，与宾语 castles 之间是逻辑上的被动关系）【P41】

②The peaceful landscape of the "Emerald Isle" and its many green counties is a true feast for the eyes, with its rolling green hills **dotted with sheep and cattle**. "绿宝石岛"风光宁静秀美，郡县草木葱茏，青山连绵起伏，牛羊点缀其中，着实让人大饱眼福。（过去分词短语 dotted with sheep and cattle 作宾补，与宾语 hills 之间是逻辑上的被动关系）【P44】

### ❶ 过去分词作宾语补足语时，宾语往往是其动作的对象。

二者有逻辑上的动宾关系。过去分词作宾补，表示被动或完成意义，有时两者兼而有之。

- In the morning people woke up and found the world outside their houses completely **changed**. 早上人们醒来发现房子外面的世界完全变了。（过去分词 changed 作宾补，表示被动和完成）

### ❷ 能用过去分词作宾补的几类动词

（1）表示感觉和心理状态的感官动词或短语，常见的有 see、hear、find、notice、observe、look at、listen

to 等。感官动词后加过去分词作宾补，表示被动和完成，也可表示状态。

- Seeing our fruits of labour **packed and loaded onto** the truck, we all felt very satisfied. 看到我们的劳动成果被打包装上卡车，我们所有人都感到很满意。
- He **found** the room **decorated with** flowered wallpaper. 他发现房间用花墙纸装饰着。

**特别注意** 若这些感官动词后作宾补的动词与宾语是逻辑上的主动关系且强调动作的全过程，应用省略 to 的不定式；若是逻辑上的主动关系且正在进行应用现在分词形式。

- I **heard** her **sing** an English song just now. 我刚才听到她唱英文歌了。（宾语 her 与动词 sing 之间是逻辑上的主动关系，表示听的全过程，所以用 do 的形式作宾补）
- I **heard** her **singing** an English song when I passed by. 我路过的时候听到她在唱英文歌。（宾语 her 与动词 sing 之间是逻辑上的主动关系，表示正在进行，所以用 doing 的形式作宾补）
- When he came to life, he **found** himself **sitting** on a chair, with his hands tied back. 他苏醒过来之后，发现自己坐在椅子上，双手被反绑在背后。

（2）表示"使；令；让"等意义的使役动词，常见的有 have、make、get、keep 等。

①have sth done 常用含义为：（主语）请/派别人完成某事；（主语）遭受某种不幸的情况。

- I'm going to **have my letters posted** tomorrow if I've got them ready by then. 如果明天我能将信函准备好的话，到时我就把它们寄出去。
- He **had his leg broken** in the match yesterday. 他在昨天的比赛中摔断了腿。

②make 常用于 make oneself heard/understood/

---

---

<!-- page-096 -->


known 表达中，意为"让人听到/理解/知道自己"。

- Being poor at English, I'm afraid I can't **make myself understood** in English. 由于我的英语很差劲，我怕我不能用英语让别人理解我的意思。

③get sth done = have sth done 常用含义为：（主语）让别人完成某事。

- I must **get my bike repaired**. 我必须请人修理我的自行车。

④keep 可用于 keep sb/sth done 结构，意为"使某人/某事被……"。

- Michael put up a picture of Yao Ming beside the bed to **keep himself reminded** of his own dreams. 迈克尔在他的床边贴了一张姚明的照片以此提醒自己不要忘了自己的梦想。

**特别注意** 若作宾补的动词与宾语是逻辑上的主动关系时：

①have sb do sth 让某人做某事

have sb doing sth 让某人一直做某事（处于某种状态）

- The teacher **had** us **make up** conversations during classes. 老师让我们在课堂上编对话。（宾语 us 和宾补 make up 之间是逻辑上的主动关系）
- I can't **have** you **running** up and down all day long. 我不能让你整天跑上跑下。（宾语 you 和宾补 run 之间是逻辑上的主动关系，用 doing 的形式来表示一直持续的状态。在否定句中 have 还可以当"容忍"讲）

②make sb do sth 让某人做某事

- Nothing, but nothing would **make him change** his mind. 没有什么，绝对没有什么会使他改变主意。

③get sb to do 让某人去做某事

get sb doing sth 让某人一直做某事（强调一直持续的状态）

- The teacher **got** the students **to look up** all the words they didn't know. 老师让学生们去查所有他们不认识的单词。（宾语 students 和宾补 look up 之间是逻辑上的主动关系）

- It is hard to **get him doing** his homework before dinner every day. 让他每天晚饭前做作业是很困难的。

④keep sb doing sth 让某人一直做某事（强调动作延续一段时间）

- Sorry to **keep** you **waiting**. I had an important meeting and it ran over. 对不起，让你们久等了。我有个重要会议，会议超时了。

（3）表示"希望；愿望；命令"的动词或短语，常见的有 want、wish、would like、expect、order 等。

- He **wanted** his ability **recognised**. 他希望他的能力得到认可。
- The boss **wouldn't like** the problem **discussed** at the moment. 老板不希望现在讨论这个问题。

**特别注意** 若作宾补的动词与宾语是逻辑上的主动关系时，应用不定式形式。

- You can hardly **expect** her **to do** it for free. 你不可能指望她无偿地做这件事。

### ❸ 过去分词用在"with+宾语+宾补"结构中

- The living room is clean and tidy, **with a dinning table already laid** for a meal to be cooked. 客厅干净整洁，一张准备放即将做好的饭菜的餐桌已经摆好了。
- You have no idea how she finished the relay race **with her foot wounded** so much. 你不知道她是怎样在脚受伤如此严重的情况下完成接力赛的。

**特别注意** 在 with 复合结构中，若作宾补的动词与宾语之间是逻辑上的主动关系且表示动作正在进行，则用现在分词形式；若作宾补的动词表示将来的动作，则用不定式形式。

- **With so many people looking at me**, I felt nervous. 这么多人看着我，我感到很紧张。
- **With a lot of homework to do**, I can't go out with you. 有很多作业要做，我不能和你出去。

> 音乐歌颂人们的生活，引导人们走向光明的未来。

## 探究词汇 提高

## perform *vi. & vt.* 表演 *vt.* 履行；做；执行 *vi.* 运转

**释义**

①to entertain an audience by playing a piece of music, acting in a play, etc

②to do sth, such as a piece of work, task or duty

③to work or function well or badly

**教材原句 P52** Virtual choir members record themselves while they **perform** alone on video. "虚拟合唱团"的成员在独自演唱时录制视频。

**常用搭配** perform an operation/experiment 做手术/实验

perform an important role in… 在……中发挥着重要的作用

perform well/badly/poorly 表现/运转得好/不好/糟糕

**联想词** ①performer *n.* 表演者

②performance *n.*［C］表演；演出［U，*sing.*］履行；执行

give/put on a performance 演出

## prove *vt.* 证明；展现 *linking v.* 证明是；被发现是

**教材原句 P52** It has **proved** to be a positive

influence on the lives of many people. 事实证明，这对许多人的生活产生了积极的影响。

- The book has **proved** very popular, giving students motivation and a sense of achievement. 这本书被证明很受欢迎，它给了学生们动力和成就感。

**常见用法** prove that… 证明……

prove（to be）sth 证明（是）……

prove oneself（to be）sth 证明自己（是）……

it is proved that… 据证明……

- He **proved（to be）an honest friend**. 事实表明，他是一位诚实的朋友。
- He **proved himself（to be）an amusing companion**. 他证明自己是位很有趣的伙伴。

**联想词** proof *n.* 证明；证据

there's（no）proof that…（没）有证据证明……

## award *n.*［C］奖；奖品 *vt.* 授予；给予奖励；判给

**教材原句 P52** The virtual choir was the idea of **award**-winning composer and conductor Eric Whitacre. "虚拟合唱团"的构想来源于获奖作曲家兼指挥家埃里克·惠特克。

**常用搭配** win an award of… 赢得……奖

award sb sth＝award sth to sb 授予/给予某人某物

- The British Government **awarded him a grant of** £1,500. 英国政府给予他 1,500 英镑的补助金。

**易混辨析** **award、reward 与 prize 的用法区别**

| 易混词 | 用法 |
| --- | --- |
| award | 指因某种突出的成就而由官方颁发的"奖赏或荣誉"。● He was nominated for the best actor **award**. 他获得最佳男演员奖提名。 |
| reward | 指为某事作出贡献而得到的"回报；报酬；赏金"。● My grandmother says that a good deed is its own **reward**. 我祖母说善举本身就是回报。 |
| prize | 指因在某方面作出巨大贡献或在比赛、竞赛中胜出而获得的"奖品；奖项"。● I won first **prize**! 我得了一等奖！ |

## relief *n.*（不快过后的）宽慰、轻松或解脱；（焦虑、痛苦等的）减轻或消除；救济品

**教材原句 P56** Music gave me strength and brought me **relief**. 音乐给予我力量，并让我感到轻松。

**常用搭配** with relief 欣慰地

in relief 如释重负；松了口气

to one's relief 令某人欣慰的是

breathe a sigh of relief 轻松地舒口气；松一口气

- **In relief** Daisy burst into laughter. 黛西如释重负，突然大笑了起来。
- No one was hurt, and we all **breathed a sigh of relief**. 没有人受伤，我们都松了一口气。[《朗文当代》]

**联想词** relieve *vt.* 减轻；缓解

relieve sb of… 帮某人减轻……（负担）

- A secretary was hired to **relieve her of** some of the administrative work. 一个秘书被雇来为她减轻一些行政工作的负担。[《朗文当代》]

## cure

### ❶ *vt.* 治愈；治好（疾病）；解决（问题）

**教材原句 P56** Two years ago, I was told I had a serious disease which was difficult to **cure**. 两年前，我被告知自己患有一种难以治愈的严重疾病。

**常用搭配** cure sb of sth 治愈某人的某种疾病；改正某人的某种不良行为

- Now doctors believe they have **cured him of** the disease. 现在医生们认为他们已经治好了他的病。
- You should **cure** your son **of** his carelessness. 你应该纠正你儿子粗心的毛病。

### ❷ *n.* 药物；治疗；（解决问题、改善糟糕情况的）措施

**常用搭配** a cure for… ……的疗法；……的对策

- There is no **cure for** the disease, although drugs can slow down its rate of development. 尽管药物可以减缓病情的发展速度，但是这种病仍然是不治之症。
- a **cure for** poverty 解决贫穷问题的措施

---

---

<!-- page-100 -->


## absorbed in sth/sb 被……吸引住；专心致志

**教材原句 P54** When he got **absorbed in** his world of music, he felt as if he could "see" the beauty of the world around him, like he had in his previous life. 当他完全投入他的音乐世界时，他觉得自己仿佛可以"看到"他周围美丽的世界，就像他以前（看得见）的日子一样。

**联想拓展** ①absorb *vt.* 理解；掌握；吸收（热、光、能等）；使全神贯注

- I really don't have time to **absorb** everything that he said. 我真的没有时间弄懂他说的一切。
- A transparent object doesn't **absorb** or scatter light, at least not very much. 一个透明的物体不会吸收或者散射光，至少不会太多。

②其他表示"全神贯注于……；专心于……"的短语：be buried in、be devoted to、be lost in、put one's heart into。

## aim

### ❶ *vi. & vt.* 力求达到，力争做到；瞄准 *vt.* 目的是；旨在

**教材原句 P54** Filled with team spirit, they act as a whole, always **aiming for** glory. 他们富有团队精神，团结一致，总是向着光荣进发。

**常用搭配** aim to do sth 力争做到某事

aim at/for 瞄准；力争做到

be aimed at doing sth 旨在做某事

- In a word, the programme **aims to encourage** students to be active citizens and engage themselves in making a difference in society. 总之，这个计划力求鼓励学生们成为积极的市民并让他们在社会中发挥作用。
- This activity **is aimed at improving** the students' ability to listen and speak. 这项活动的目的是提高学生的听说能力。

### ❷ *n.* 目的；目标

- The **aim** of education is to teach young people to think for themselves and not follow others blindly.

教育的目的是教会年轻人独立思考，不盲目追随他人。

**常用搭配** with the aim of… 目的是……

achieve one's aim 达到某人的目的

take aim at… 把目标对准……；把批评的矛头指向……

## set sth up 安装好（设备或机器）；建立；开办

**教材原句 P55** people to **set up** equipment 设备安装人员

- Many countries are now **setting up** national parks where animals and plants can be protected. 很多国家现在都在建立能够保护动植物的国家公园。

**联想拓展** 含有 set 的其他常用短语

set aside 将……放在一边；节省或保留（钱或时间）

set down 写下；记下；制定（规则、原则等）

set off 出发；启程；使爆炸　set fire to 放火烧……

set about 着手/开始（做……）（后接名词或动名词）

set out 动身踏上；开始（做……）（后接动词不定式）

set back 使推迟；使延误

set an example for sb 为某人树立榜样

- She **sets aside** a bit of money every month. 她每个月都省下来一点钱。
- As soon as she arrived, she **set about tidying** the room. 她一到，就开始整理房间。
- The moment I got home, I **set out to do** my homework. 我一到家，就开始做家庭作业。

## equipment *n.*［U］设备；装备

**教材原句 P55** people to set up **equipment** 设备安装人员

**常用搭配** office equipment 办公设备

a piece of equipment 一件设备

**联想词** equip *vt.* 装备；配备；使有所准备；使能胜任

be equipped with 配有……；装有……

be equipped to do 配有装备以做……

equip sb for 使某人为……做好准备；使某人能够胜任……

---

---

<!-- page-101 -->


- Homes **are equipped with** voice controlled devices for lighting and temperature. 房屋配备了用于照明和调节温度的声控设备。
- As far as I'm concerned, education is about learning and the more you learn, the more **equipped for** life you are. 就我而言，教育就是学习，你学的越多，你为生活做的准备就越充分。

## assume *vt.* 认为；假设；取得（权力）；承担（责任）；就（职）

**释义**

①to think or accept that sth is true but without having proof of it

②to take or begin to have power or responsibility

**教材原句 P55** So can I **assume** that the aim of the festival is to raise money? 所以我可以认为举办这个音乐节是为了筹集资金吗？

- Even **assuming** this to be in principle possible, it will not be achieved soon. 即使假定这在理论上行得通，它也不会很快实现。
- Everyone must **assume his share of the responsibility** to protect the environment. 每个人都必须承担起保护环境的责任。

**常见用法** it is assumed that… 人们认为……

- It's generally **assumed that** reading enables us to acquire knowledge and broaden our horizons. 人们普遍认为阅读能让我们获得知识并拓宽视野。

**联想词** ①assumption *n.* 假定；假设

make assumptions about… 作关于……的推测/假设

on the assumption that… 假设……

②assuming（that）*conj.* 假定；假设

## in addition（to sb/sth） 除……以外（还）

**教材原句 P55** How else could you help **in addition to** what has already been mentioned? 除了已经提到的内容以外，你还能帮忙做些什么呢？

**易混辨析** **in addition to 与 in addition 的用法区别**

| 易混短语 | 含义及用法 |
| --- | --- |
| in addition to | "除……之外（还）"，与 as well as 同义，为介词短语，其后应接名词、代词、动名词等。 |
| in addition | "另外"，相当于副词，通常可以和 furthermore、besides 等互换。 |

- **In addition to** his movie work, Redford is known as a champion of environmental causes. 除了拥有电影事业，雷德福还是一位公认的环保卫士。
- The company provides cheap Internet access. **In addition**, it makes shareware freely available. 该公司提供廉价的上网服务。此外，它还让人们免费使用共享软件。

**联想拓展** addition *n.* 添加；加法；增加物

## treatment *n.* 治疗；对待；处理

**教材原句 P56** Then one day, I had to go through a two-hour medical **treatment**. 后来有一天，我要接受两个小时的医疗。

- The wastewater **treatment** facilities have been constructed, which has helped improve the water quality and water conservation. 污水处理设施已经建成，这有助于改善水质和节约用水。

**联想词** treat

①*vt.* 治疗；对待；款待

treat sb for… 医治某人……病

treat sb as/like… 把某人当作……对待

treat sb/oneself to sth 用某物招待/款待某人（自己）

- She left the hospital and **treated herself to** a box of doughnuts. 她离开了医院，并且给自己买了一盒甜甜圈。

②*n.* 款待；招待；乐事；乐趣

- You don't have to pay for dinner. It's my **treat**. 晚餐你不用付钱，我请客。
- When we were kids, a trip to the beach was a real **treat**. 我们小时候，到沙滩去玩是件特别开心的事。

---

---

<!-- page-102 -->


## get through 熬过（困难时期）；渡过（难关）；（设法）处理；完成；（用电话）联系上；通过

**教材原句 P56** It was the rock I leant on to become strong and to **get through** those hard times. 摇滚乐是我的依靠，它支撑着我，让我变得坚强，并熬过那段艰难的时光。

- I tried calling you several times but I couldn't **get through**. 我试着给你打了几次电话，但都没打通。
- Luckily, Jack **got through** the driving test at the first attempt. 幸运的是，杰克一次就通过了驾驶考试。

**联想拓展** 含有 get 的其他常用短语

get across 解释清楚；传达

get along/on 进展；相处融洽

get away 外出度假；离开；逃脱

get away with 做（坏事）而未受到惩罚

get down to sth 开始做某事；开始认真对待某事

get over 克服（困难）；从（疾病或不愉快的经历等）中恢复常态

- He's not very good at **getting** his ideas **across**. 他不太擅长清楚地表达自己的想法。
- I had hoped to take a holiday this year but I wasn't able to **get away**. 我本来想今年休假，但是我走不开。
- After Christmas I'm going to **get down to** some serious job-hunting. 圣诞节过后我打算开始认真地找工作。
- He was disappointed at not getting the job, but he'll **get over** it. 他因没有得到那份工作而大失所望，不过他会想得开的。

## satisfaction *n.*［U］满足；满意；欣慰［C］令人满意的事（物）

**教材原句 P56** Moreover, music gave me hope and a sense of **satisfaction**. 此外，音乐给了我希望和满足感。

- The happy news was a **satisfaction** to us all. 这个好消息使我们大家都感到满意。

**常用搭配** with satisfaction 满意地

to sb's satisfaction 令某人满意的是

a sense of satisfaction 满足感

- Sarah looked at the finished painting **with satisfaction**. 萨拉满意地看着这幅成品画。
- The question could not be resolved, at least not **to my satisfaction**. 这个问题无法解决，至少达不到让我满意的地步。

**联想词** ①satisfy *vt.* 使满足；使满意

②satisfied *adj.*（感到）满意的

be satisfied with… 对……感到满意

- There are so many people who **are satisfied with** the products made in the company. 有那么多人对这家公司制造的产品感到满意。

③satisfying *adj.* 令人满意的；令人高兴的

④satisfactory *adj.* 令人满意的；合适的

## various *adj.* 各种不同的；各种各样的

**教材原句 P56** At the same time, we all go through **various** periods when we feel sad or alone. 与此同时，我们总会经历各种悲伤或孤独的时期。

**联想词** ①variety *n.* 种类；多样化

a variety of/varieties of 各种各样的

- The shirt is available in a wide **variety of** colours. 这种衬衫有各种各样的颜色可供选择。

②vary *vi.* 不同；（根据情况）变化，变更，改变

vary from… to… 从……到……之间变化

vary with… 随着……而变化

- The heights of the plants **vary from** 8 cm **to** 20 cm. 这些植物的高度从 8 厘米到 20 厘米不等。
- Her mood **varies with** the weather. 她的情绪随天气而变化。

## reaction *n.* 反应；回应；（药物或食物引起的）不良反应

**教材原句 P58** Encouraged by this first performance and the positive **reaction** of the

audience, I have continued to play the piano and enjoy it more every day. 首次演出的经历以及观众积极的回应给了我极大的鼓舞，我继续弹钢琴，每天都享受着它带给我的与日俱增的乐趣。

**常用搭配** reaction to… 对……的反应

- Some people may have a bad **reaction to** those chemicals when they eat the crops. 食用这些农作物时，有人可能会对那些化学物质产生不良反应。

**联想词** react *vi.*（作出）反应；回应；起化学反应

react with… 与……发生化学反应

react to… 对……作出反应/回应

- How do you think John will **react to** her? 你认为约翰将怎样回应她？
- Workers **reacted** angrily **to** the news of more job losses at the factory. 工人们对工厂进一步裁员的消息感到气愤。

**差距词汇 拔尖**

**passion** *n.* 酷爱；热衷的爱好（或活动等）

Although I've been learning Western instruments, I have a **passion for** Chinese folk music. 虽然我一直在学习西方乐器，但我酷爱中国民间音乐。

**skip** *vi.* 蹦蹦跳跳地走；跳绳 *vt. & vi.* 略过

These people who wore beautiful clothes **skipped** back and forth to the rhythm of music in the hall. 这些穿着漂亮衣服的人随着音乐的节奏在大厅里跳来跳去。

**categorise** *vt.* 将……分类

The app offers the best music videos **categorised** by the music video type and artist name. 这款应用程序提供按音乐视频类型和艺人名字分类的最佳音乐视频。

**comedy** *n.* 喜剧，喜剧片

It is hard to name a **comedy** actor who hasn't been on the stage here. 很难找出一个没有在这里登台的喜剧演员。

**live** *adj.* 现场演出的

It presents **live** entertainment, including pop, rock, folk, jazz, musicals, dance, world music, films and classical music. 它提供现场娱乐表演，包括流行音乐、摇滚、民谣、爵士乐、音乐剧、舞蹈、世界音乐、电影和古典音乐。

**melodious** *adj.* 悦耳的；旋律优美的；动听的

With the **melodious** song coming to an end, hearty applause burst out. 随着悠扬的歌声接近尾声，全场爆发出热烈的掌声。

# Part Ⅱ　句子分类探究

## 长难句分析

> **A virtual choir enables them to add their voices to those of other individuals and become part of the global community.**

（主语：A virtual choir；谓语：enables；宾语：them；并列的不定式短语作宾补：to add their voices to those of other individuals and（to）become part of the global community）【P52】

**句意** "虚拟合唱团"让他们能够将自己的声音与其他人的声音相融合，并成为全球社区的一部分。

**知识拓展** **one、the one、that、those 的指代用法**

在句子中为了避免重复，常用替代词 one、the one、that 或 those 来替代名词。

one 表泛指，指代上文提到的那一类事物中的"任意一个"。

the one 表特指，指代上文提到的或者特指的"那

---

---

<!-- page-104 -->


that 与 the one 意思虽然相同，也是特指同类事物的"那一个"。但 that 也可替代不可数名词，而 the one 则不能。

those 是 that 的复数形式，指代前面出现的复数名词，相当于 the ones。

- These books are rather difficult for me. Please get me some easier **ones**. 这些书对我来说太难了，请给我一些容易的（书）。
- This TV set is much more expensive than **that/the one** in the window. 这台电视机比橱窗里的那台（电视机）贵多了。
- Weather here is much hotter than **that** in Beijing. 这儿的天气比北京的（天气）热多了。
- The oranges in the box are better than **those** on the shelf. 盒子里的橙子比架子上的（橙子）好。

> **During those times, music can help you in the same way that it helped me.**

（时间状语：During those times；主句：music can help you；that 引导定语从句，修饰先行词 the same way：that it helped me）【P56】

**句意** 在那样的日子里，音乐可以像帮助我一样帮到你们。

**知识拓展** 先行词是 way 时，其后定语从句的关系词的选择依情况而定：

（1）如果定语从句中不缺主语、宾语或表语，则定语从句用 in which 或 that 引导，关系词也可省略。

- Please tell me the **way（in which/that）** you worked out the maths problem. 请告诉我你算出这道数学题的方法。

（2）如果定语从句中缺少主语、宾语或表语，则定语从句应用 that 或 which 引导。关系词如在从句中作宾语，也可将其省略。

- I think the **way（that/which）** he told me is best. 我认为他告诉我的那个方法是最好的。

## 高频句式

**句式 1　as 引导定语从句 正如……**

as 引导非限制性定语从句，从句常含有这些动词：see、know、hear、watch、remember、say、tell、show、expect、guess 等，这类动词与 as 连用几乎成了一种固定搭配。as 引导的此类从句可以置于句首、句中或句尾，as 在从句中表示"正如；如……所……"。

**链接课文** **As one virtual choir member said**, "Music helps me to… forget my problems…" 正如一位"虚拟合唱团"成员所说的："音乐帮助我……忘记自己的问题……"【P52】

**经典例句**

- **As Picasso said**, action is the foundation. 正如毕加索所说，行动是（一切的）基石。
- **As I remember**, there is a bookshop around here. 我记得这附近有一家书店。

**句式 2　it's an honour to do sth 很荣幸做某事**

it 作形式主语，不定式短语作真正的主语。

**知识拓展** it's an honour/a pity/a shame/a fact/a surprise（that）… 意为"……很荣幸/真可惜/真丢人/是事实/令人惊讶"，it 为形式主语，that 从句为真正的主语。

**链接课文** **It's an honour to be here and to share** with you the story of how music has had an impact on my life. 我很荣幸能够在这里与你们分享音乐是如何影响我的生活的。[P56]

**经典例句**

- **It is a great honour for us to be invited** here to meet the great scientist face to face. 受邀来这里和这位伟大的科学家面对面交流，对我们来说是莫大的荣幸。
- **It is a pity that** in the story nobody helped the dodo. 很遗憾故事中没有人帮助那只渡渡鸟。

---

---

<!-- page-105 -->


# Part Ⅲ　单元语法归纳

## 重点1　过去分词作表语

**教材原句**

When he got **absorbed** in his world of music, he felt as if he could "see" the beauty of the world around him, like he had in his previous life. 当他完全投入他的音乐世界时，他觉得自己仿佛可以"看到"他周围美丽的世界，就像他以前（看得见）的日子一样。（过去分词 absorbed 作表语，表示主语 he 所处的状态）【P54】

过去分词作表语，表示主语的感受或所处的状态，系动词除了 be 动词外，常用的还有：become、get、remain、stay、seem 等。

### ❶ 过去分词与现在分词作表语的区别

作表语的过去分词和现在分词，大部分已被形容词化。过去分词作表语多表示主语自身的感受或主语所处的状态，表示"（感到）……的"。现在分词作表语多表示主语所具有的特征，表示"令人……的"。get、become、look、seem、appear 等连系动词后面常跟过去分词作表语，表示被动或主语的状态。

- The film was so **moving** that all of us were **moved** to tears. 这部电影如此感人以至于我们都感动得哭了。
- The injured dog seems **frightened** in that cave. Let's go and see. 那只受伤的狗在那个山洞里似乎很害怕。咱们去看看吧。

### ❷ 过去分词作表语与动词被动语态的区别

当"be+过去分词"表示主语所处的状态时为系表结构，be 是连系动词，be 后面的过去分词是表语，相当于形容词。

- When I went in, they **were absorbed** in a heated discussion. 当我进去的时候，他们正全神贯注地进行热烈的讨论。（系表结构）

当"be+过去分词"表示动作时为被动语态结构，be 是助动词，be 后面的过去分词是主要动词，动作的对象是主语。

- It is one of the most beautiful cities in the world and **is visited** by more than eight million tourists every year. 它是世界上最美丽的城市之一，每年有超过八百万游客游览参观。（被动语态）

## 重点2　过去分词作状语

**教材原句**

①**Born in the USA on 2 January 1970**, Whitacre began studying music at the University of Nevada in 1988. 惠特克 1970 年 1 月 2 日出生在美国，1988 年开始在内华达大学学习音乐。（过去分词短语作伴随状语）【P52】

②**Moved by this music**, he said, "It was like seeing color for the first time." 他被这段音乐打动了，他说："那种（兴奋的）感觉就像第一次看到色彩一样。"（过去分词短语作原因状语，move 与主语之间为逻辑上的被动关系）【P52】

③**Inspired**, he asked his fans to make videos, which he then joined together into one performance. 他受到启发，让他的歌迷们制作视频，然后他把这些视频组合成一场表演。（过去分词 Inspired 作原因状语，inspire 与主语之间是逻辑上的被动关系）【P52】

过去分词作状语时，表示完成、被动，其逻辑主语是整个句子的主语，其对应的动词与逻辑主语之间存在着被动关系。过去分词作状语可表示时间、原因、条件、让步、方式、伴随等。

### ❶ 过去分词作时间状语

过去分词作时间状语，可转化为时间状语从句。有时也可在过去分词前加上连词 when、while、until 等，使其意义更加明确。

---

---

<!-- page-106 -->


- **Translated into English**, the sentence was found to have an entirely different word order.＝**When translated into English**, the sentence was found to have an entirely different word order. 翻译成英语后，我们发现这个句子的语序全变了。
- There are some health problems that, **when not treated in time**, can become bigger ones later on. 有一些健康问题，若不及时处理，今后会成为更大的问题。
- Film has a much shorter history, **especially when compared to** such art forms as music and painting. 电影制作的历史要短得多，尤其与音乐和绘画等艺术形式比起来。

### ❷ 过去分词作原因状语

过去分词作原因状语，可转换为原因状语从句。

- **Raised in the poorest area of Glasgow**, he had a long, hard road to becoming a football star.＝Because/Since/As he was raised in the poorest area of Glasgow, he had a long, hard road to becoming a football star. 因为在格拉斯哥最贫穷的地区长大，他的球星之路是漫长而艰难的。
- **Absorbed in painting**, John didn't notice evening approaching. 由于专注于绘画，约翰没有注意到天快黑了。

### ❸ 过去分词作条件状语

过去分词作条件状语，可转换为条件状语从句。

- **Given more attention**, the cabbages could have grown better.＝If they had been given more attention, the cabbages could have grown better. 如果它们被给予更多的关注，这些洋白菜还可以长得更好。

### ❹ 过去分词作让步状语

过去分词作让步状语，有时可用在 although、though、even if、even though 等连词之后，可转换为让步状语从句。

- **Though warned of the storm**, the farmers were still working in the fields.＝Though they had been

**warned of the storm**, the farmers were still working in the fields. 尽管被提醒将有暴风雨，农民们仍然在地里干活。

### ❺ 过去分词作方式或伴随状语

过去分词作方式或伴随状语，句子可转换为由 and 连接的并列句。

- Robert has taken temporary control of team matters, **supported by his two assistants**. 罗伯特在两名助手的支持下，暂时接管了团队事务。

### ❻ 过去分词作状语注意要点

（1）过去分词作状语时，过去分词的逻辑主语与整个句子的主语一致。如果其逻辑主语与整个句子的主语不一致，则需要用独立主格结构或 with 复合结构。

- **Weather permitting**, we'll go for a picnic tomorrow. 天气允许的话，我们明天就去野餐。
- **With his homework finished**, he went out to play. 作业完成后，他出去玩了。

（2）有些过去分词源于系表结构，作状语时不强调被动，而重在描述主语的状态。常见的有：satisfied、surprised、worried、disappointed、lost、seated、dressed in、determined、located in、tired of、devoted to、born 等。

- **Dressed in blue trousers and red braces**, the round-faced Lee is every inch a hands-on restaurateur. 身着蓝色长裤和红色背带，圆脸的李先生俨然是一位亲力亲为的餐馆老板。
- **Born in this beautiful town**, he hates to leave it. 出生于这个美丽的小镇，他不愿离开这里。

### ❼ 过去分词和现在分词作状语的区别

| 过去分词作状语 | 现在分词作状语 |
| --- | --- |
| 表示被动的动作，与其逻辑主语之间为被动关系。 | 表示主动的动作，与其逻辑主语之间为主动关系。 |
| 表示已经完成的动作，动作通常发生在谓语动词所表示的动作之前。 | 表示正在进行的动作，与谓语动词所表示的动作同时或几乎同时发生。 |
